Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on structures structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 572. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 644 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by structures generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 644. QED.
